[AMZN_Q2_2024] Worldwide revenue was $148 billion, an 11% increase year over year, excluding the impact of foreign exchange.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] Worldwide operating income nearly doubled year over year to $14.7 billion, which was $700 million above the high end of our guidance range.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] North America segment revenue was $90 billion, an increase of 9% year over year, and international segment revenue was $31.7 billion, an increase of 10% year over year, excluding the impact of foreign exchange.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] North America segment operating income was $5.1 billion, an increase of $1.9 billion year over year.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] Our international segment was profitable again in Q2, with operating income of $300 million, an improvement of $1.2 billion year over year.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] Revenue was $26.3 billion, an increase of 18.8% year over year, excluding the impact of foreign exchange.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] AWS now is an annualized revenue run rate of more than $105 billion.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] AWS operating income was $9.3 billion, an increase of $4 billion year over year.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] For the first half of the year, capex was $30.5 billion.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] However, if you actually deliver too much capacity, the economics are pretty woeful and you don't like the returns of the operating income.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] Today, we're reporting $148 billion in revenue, up 11% year over year, excluding the impact of foreign exchange rates.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] Operating income was $14.7 billion, up 91% year over year, and trailing 12-month free cash flow adjusted for equipment finance leases was $51.4 billion, up 664% or $44.7 billion year over year.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] On the AWS question, it's always hard to predict what the growth rates are going to be, and it's a relatively large business at $105 billion revenue run rate at this point.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] I think the one other thing I would say about that, too, Mark, is the business today, as I mentioned, it's a $105 billion revenue run rate business, about 90% of the global IT spend is still on-premises.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] So, the drop in revenue sequentially -- revenue growth sequentially was tied to ASP and both continuation of existing trends, but also as we talked about growth in our everyday essentials business and categories.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] For perspective, we've added over $2 billion in advertising revenue year over year and generated more than $50 billion in revenue in the trailing 12 months.
[10-K FY2024 — Item 7. Management’s Discussion and Analysis of Financial Condition and Results of Operations] Cash provided by (used in) investing activities corresponds with cash capital expenditures, including leasehold improvements, incentives received from property and equipment vendors, proceeds from asset sales, cash outlays for 22 Table of Contents acquisitions, investments in other companies and intellectual property rights, and purchases, sales, and maturities of marketable securities.
[10-K FY2024 — Item 7. Management’s Discussion and Analysis of Financial Condition and Results of Operations] Cash provided by (used in) investing activities was $(49.8) billion and $(94.3) billion in 2023 and 2024, with the variability caused primarily by purchases, sales, and maturities of marketable securities and cash capital expenditures.
[10-K FY2024 — Item 7. Management’s Discussion and Analysis of Financial Condition and Results of Operations] Cash capital expenditures were $48.1 billion, and $77.7 billion in 2023 and 2024, which primarily reflect investments in technology infrastructure (the majority of which is to support AWS business growth) and in additional capacity to support our fulfillment network.
[AMZN_Q2_2024] Today, we're reporting $148 billion in revenue, up 11% year over year, excluding the impact of foreign exchange rates.
